Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The ecology variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 110 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 900 and intersection form of rank 4.

This completes the proof. QED.
